   Sunny Optical Technology (Group) Company Limited
   舜 宇 光 學 科 技（ 集 團) 有 限 公 司




2022   Annual Report
       年報
CORPORATE PROFILE
公司簡介
Sunny Optical Technology (Group) Company Limited (the “Company” or “Sunny Optical
Technology”, together with its subsidiaries, the “Group”) (Stock Code: 2382.HK) is a global
leading integrated optical components and products manufacturer. The Group is principally
engaged in the design, research and development (the “R&D”), manufacture and sales of
optical and optical-related products. Such products include optical components (such as
vehicle lens sets, optical parts of vehicle light detection and ranging (“LiDAR”), virtual reality
(“VR”) positioning lens sets, handset lens sets, glass spherical lenses for digital cameras
and other optical components) (the “Optical Components”), optoelectronic products (such
as vehicle modules, VR folded path (“Pancake”) modules, VR visual modules, handset
camera modules and other optoelectronic modules) (the “Optoelectronic Products”) and
optical instruments (such as intelligent inspection equipment and microscopes) (the “Optical
Instruments”). The Group focuses on the application fields of optoelectronic-related products,
such as vehicles, VR/augmented reality (“AR”), robots and handsets, which are combined with
optical, electronic, algorithm and mechanical technologies.



舜宇光學科技（集團）有限公司（「本公司」或「舜宇光學科技」，連同其附屬公司「本集團」）   （股份代
號：2382.HK）為一間全球領先的綜合光學零件及產品生產商。本集團主要從事設計、研究與開
發（「研發」）、生產及銷售光學及光學相關產品。該等產品包括光學零件（例如車載鏡頭、車載激
光雷達（「激光雷達」）光學部件、虛擬現實（「VR」）空間定位鏡頭、手機鏡頭、數碼相機玻璃球面
鏡片及其他光學零部件）  （「光學零件」）、光電產品（例如車載模組、VR折疊光路（「Pancake」）模
塊、VR視覺模組、手機攝像模組及其他光電模組）   （「光電產品」）及光學儀器（例如智能檢測設備
及顯微鏡） （「光學儀器」）。本集團專注於結合光學、電子、算法及機械技術的光電相關產品的應
用領域：如汽車、VR╱ 增強現實（「AR」）、機器人及手機。

QUANTITATIVE AND QUALITATIVE                                                 市場風險的量化和質化披露
DISCLOSURE ABOUT MARKET RISK

Interest Rate Risk                                                           利率風險

The Group is exposed to interest rate risks arising from its bank            本集團面對作為營運資金以及用於本集團拓展
borrowings for working capital and capital expenditure that are              和其他用途的資本開支的銀行借貸利率風險。
associated with the expansion of the Group and utilisation for other         利率的上調會增加現有及新增債務之成本。於
purposes. The rising of interest rates increases the costs of both           二零二二年十二月三十一日，固定利率銀行借

Selection Criteria                                                                   甄選標準

In determining the suitability of a candidate, the Nomination Committee              在決定候選人是否適合時，提名委員會及董事
and the Board shall consider the potential contributions a candidate                 會須考慮候選人在資歷、技能、經驗、獨立性

independence and gender diversity. The Nomination Committee and                      獻。提名委員會及董事會須考慮下述甄選準則

meant to be exhaustive:


•        the candidate’s personal ethics, reputation, character and                  •   候選人的個人道德、聲譽、品格和誠信；
         integrity;


•        the candidate’s qualifications, skills, knowledge, business                 •   候選人與本集團營運相關的資歷、技能、
         judgment and experience that are relevant to the operations of the              知識、業務判斷力和經驗；
         Group;

         “Board Diversity Policy”) of the Company (as amended from                       策（「董事會成員多元化政策」）中所訂明的
         time to time);                                                                  多元化觀點；


•        the candidate’s availability including time commitment to discharge         •   候選人就履行其董事職責能投入的時間，
         his or her responsibility as a Director, including being able to                包括能投入足夠時間出席董事會會議，入
         devote sufficient time to attend Board meetings, participate in                 職引介、培訓及其他與董事會及本公司相
         induction, trainings and other Board and Company associated                     關之活動（如果將被提名為獨立非執行董事

         an independent non-executive Director will be holding his or her                市公司董事職務，提名委員會應考慮候選
         seventh (or more) listed company directorship, the Nomination                   人提供的理由以顯示他 ╱ 她能夠投放足夠

         under the Listing Rules (as amended from time to time);

CORPORATE GOVERNANCE FUNCTION                                                    企業管治職能
The Board adopted the terms of reference for the corporate                       董事會於二零一一年十二月二十二日採納企業
governance functions on 22 December 2011 in compliance with the                  管治職能職權範圍，以遵守於二零一二年四月
code provision A.2.1 of the Corporate Governance Code which was                  一日採納的企業管治守則的守則條文第 A.2.1
adopted on 1 April 2012. Pursuant to the terms of reference of the               條。根據企業管治職能職權範圍，董事會（包
corporate governance function, the Board (including independent non-             括獨立非執行董事）每年負責制定、審閱及 ╱
executive Directors) shall be responsible for developing, reviewing              或監察本公司企業管治的政策及常規；對董事
and/or monitoring the policies and practices on corporate governance             及高級管理層進行培訓及持續專業發展；以及
of the Company annually; carrying out training and continuous                    檢討及監察本公司在遵守法律及監管規定方面
professional development of Directors and senior Management; and                 的政策及常規等。董事會已履行上述企業管治
reviewing and monitoring compliance with policies and practices in               職能，且就履行企業管治職能已審閱此企業管
legal and regulatory requirements of the Company. The Board has                  治報告。
performed the above-mentioned corporate governance functions and
this corporate governance report has been reviewed by the Board in
discharge of its corporate governance functions.

Background and Vision                                                          背景與願景

The Company attaches great importance to the people-oriented                   本公司尤為重視以人為本及多元化。本公司明
and diversity in particular. The Company recognises and embraces               白並深信董事會成員多元化對提升董事會履職

Policy Statement                                                               政策聲明

The Company believes that board diversity can enhance the decision-            本公司相信董事會成員多元化能提高決策能
making capability and a diverse board is more effective in dealing             力，而且一個多元化的董事會能更有效地處理
with organisational changes and would be less likely to suffer from            組織的變化，並可較少受到群體思維的影響。
group thinking. With a view to achieving a sustainable and balanced            為達致可持續及均衡的發展，本公司視提升董
development, the Company sees increasing diversity at the Board level          事會層面的多元化為支持其達到戰略目標及其
as an essential element in supporting the attainment of its strategic          可持續發展的關鍵因素。
objectives and its sustainable development.

professional experience, business perspectives, skills, knowledge              視野、技能、知識及服務任期，以及董事會不
and service term, and any other factors that the Board may consider            時認為相關及適用的任何其他因素，並著重於
relevant and applicable from time to time. High emphasis is placed             確保董事會成員的技能及經驗組合均衡分佈，
on ensuring a balanced composition of skills and experience for the            以提供不同觀點、見解和提問，讓董事會可以
Board members in order to provide a range of perspectives, insights            有效地履行其職務及職責、就本集團的核心業
and challenges that enable the Board to discharge its duties and               務及策略制定良策，以及配合董事會的繼任計

Risk Management Policy Framework                                          風險管理政策框架

Effective risk management and internal control systems are critical       有效的風險管理及內部監控系統對實現本集團
in achieving the Group’s strategic objectives. The Group has              的戰略目標至關重要。本集團已制定風險管理
established a risk management policy framework, which was approved        政策框架，由董事會及審核委員會審核通過，
by the Board and the Audit Committee, and executed by the Risk            並由風險管理工作小組執行，授權本集團採用
Management Group. It authorizes the Group to take a consistent            貫徹一致及有效的方針，充分管理與戰略、財
and effective approach applied across the Group to fully manage the       務、運營、市場及合規相關的風險。
risks associated with strategies, financing, operations, market and
compliance.

To establish the model for the three lines of defense of risk             根據反虛假財務報告委員會下屬的發起人委員
management according to the Integrated Framework of the Committee         會（「COSO」）整合框架，建立風險管理三道防
of Sponsoring Organizations of the Treadway Commission (“COSO”):          線模型：


First line of defense: Operational departments of each company and        第一道防線：各公司運營部門及本集團職能部門
functional departments of the Group
The first line of defense mainly conducts collection of initial           第一道防線主要進行風險初始信息收集和風險
risk information and risk identification, proactively implements          識別、積極實施風險應對方案，參與風險管理
risk response plans and participates in the construction of risk          文化建設。
management culture.


Second line of defense: Risk Management Group                             第二道防線：風險管理工作小組
The second line of defense is mainly responsible for coordinating the     第二道防線主要負責風險管理及內部管控體系
overall planning, construction, maintenance and enhancement of the        的協調規劃及統籌、建設、維護和提升。
risk management and internal control system.


Third line of defense: Internal audit department                          第三道防線：內部審計部
The third line of defense is mainly responsible for examining, auditing   第三道防線主要負責對第一及第二道防線的全
and monitoring the overall risk management work of the first line and     面風險管理工作進行稽查、審計和監察。
second line of defense.

Phase 1                                                                          第一階段
Goal Setting                                                                     目標設定
The Board establishes common risk assessment criteria and sets up                董事會為本集團設定通用的風險評估準則及制
risk reference tables for the Group. Meanwhile, Risk Management                  定風險參照列表。同時，本集團風險管理工作
Group of the Group and Risk Management Group of each business                    小組和各事業分部風險管理工作小組討論並確
segment discuss and formulate overall risk management objectives,                定風險管理總體目標、管控要求與組織方式。
management requirements and organization methods.


Phase 2                                                                          第二階段
Risk Identification                                                              風險識別
Each business segment and department collects risk information                   各事業分部和部門收集與本集團相關的風險信
related to the Group and identifies the risks that potentially impact            息，並識別對其營運重要程序具有潛在影響的
the key processes of the operations. The Group also identifies                   風險。本集團亦識別改進機會。
opportunities for improvement.


Phase 3                                                                          第三階段
Risk Assessment                                                                  風險評估
Each business segment and department uses uniform and quantitative               各事業分部和部門運用統一及量化的標準，就
criteria to assess the identified risks along with their impacts on the          已識別的風險及其對業務的影響，以及其發生
business and the possibility of their occurrence to determine the risk           的可能性作出評估，確定風險等級。
level.


Phase 4                                                                          第四階段
Risk Response                                                                    風險應對
Each business segment and department determines the overall                      各事業分部和部門對評估出的風險確定總體應
response strategy and corresponding response plan for the assessed               對策略及相應應對計劃，實施風險承擔、風險
risks as well as the implementation measures such as risk taking, risk           規避、風險轉移及風險降低等措施以處理風險。
avoidance, risk transfer and risk reduction to deal with the risks.


Phase 5                                                                          第五階段
Control Activity                                                                 控制活動
Each business segment and department establishes or modifies                     各事業分部和部門建立或修訂相應制度和控制
relevant systems and control activity procedures to ensure that                  活動流程，確保採取必要的風險應對和行動計
necessary risk responses and action plans are implemented and the                劃，風險管理應對方案得以執行，以控制和管
risk management response plan is executed, in order to control and               理潛在風險。
manage potential risks.

                                                                   Corporate Governance Report
                                                                                             企業管治報告


Phase 6                                                                      第六階段
Monitoring                                                                   監控
Each business segment and department continues to follow up the              各事業分部和部門持續跟進 KRI 運行情況和風
operation situation of KRI, implementation and effectiveness of the risk     險應對計劃的實施情況及有效性，及時作出風
response plan, makes timely risk warning, adjusts response plan and          險預警，調整應對計劃，並定期向本集團內相
reports risks regularly to the relevant Management within the Group.         關管理層匯報風險。內部審計部每年獨立審閱
The internal audit department independently reviews the adequacy             風險管理體系建設的充足性及有效性，把審閱
and effectiveness of the construction of risk management system              結果及改善建議提報至審核委員會。
per annum, and submits the review results and recommendations on
improvement to the Audit Committee.


With this practical and effective framework, risk management has been        實施該實用及有效框架後，風險管理已滲入每
integrated into each functional department to resolve any possible           一個職能部門，以解決任何潛在嚴重的內部監
material internal control defect.                                            控缺失。


Group’s Risk Report                                                          本集團風險報告

The Group faces various risks and uncertainties. The relevant key            本集團面對各種風險及不確定因素，有關主要
risks are the risks that the Group must take in order to achieve its         風險乃本集團為實現其戰略目標而必須承受的
strategic objectives. If they are not handled properly, it might bring       風險，倘沒有妥善處理，可能會對本集團的運
adverse impact to the operations of the Group. A comprehensive               營帶來不利影響。全面的風險評估及風險減緩
risk assessment and risk mitigation measures help ensure possible            措施有助於確保任何潛在嚴重的內部監控缺失
material internal control defects and key risks are properly managed         及主要風險得到適當管理及有效控制，並確保
and effectively controlled, and ensure inside information is properly        根據上市規則妥善處理發佈內幕消息。以下乃
handled and disseminated in accordance with the Listing Rules. The           董事會截至二零二二年十二月三十一日止年度
followings are the principal potential risks that the Board has identified   已識別的主要潛在風險。
for the year ended 31 December 2022.

and results of various business departments of the Group, in which the            容已載於二零二二年度ESG報告中。

RESTRICTED SHARE AWARD SCHEME                                                       限制性股份獎勵計劃

On 22 March 2010 (the “Adoption Date”), the Board adopted the                       於二零一零年三月二十二日（「採納日期」），董
Restricted Share Award Scheme. Pursuant to the Restricted Share                     事會採納限制性股份獎勵計劃。根據限制性股
Award Scheme, the Directors, all employees, senior staff, agents                    份獎勵計劃，本公司及其附屬公司之董事、全
and consultants of the Company and its subsidiaries are entitled to                 體僱員、高級職員、代理及顧問均有權參與是
participate in this scheme.                                                         項計劃。


The purposes of the Restricted Share Award Scheme are to assist the                 限制性股份獎勵計劃之目的為協助本公司吸納
Company in attracting new staff as well as motivating and retaining its             新人、激勵及挽留現有人才。
existing talents.

PURCHASE, SALE OR REDEMPTION OF                                              購買、出售或贖回本公司股份
THE COMPANY’S SHARES

AND CONTINUING CONNECTED
TRANSACTIONS
The Company has established connected transaction policy to               本公司已訂立關連交易制度，以加強本公司內
strengthen internal control of the Company, protect the legitimate        部監控，保護股東合法利益，完善公司治理結
interests of the Shareholders and improve the corporate governance        構。該制度已根據上市規則定義關連人士及關
structure. This policy has defined the scopes of connected parties and    連交易的範圍，並要求本公司披露關連交易的
connected transactions in accordance with the Listing Rules. It also      相關信息，進行持續關連交易年度審核，且遵
requires the Company to disclose the related information of connected     循關連交易決策原則及迴避制度。
transactions, conduct the annual audit of continuing connected
transactions and follow the principles of the connected transactions
decision-making and abstaining rules.


The Audit Committee shall, in accordance with its roles and functions,    審核委員會根據其工作及職能，審閱關連交易
review whether the arrangements for connected transactions and            及持續關連交易的安排是否公平合理。審核委
continuing connected transactions are fair and reasonable. The Audit      員會根據客觀標準判斷該關連交易是否對本公
Committee judges whether the connected transaction is beneficial          司有利，必要時應聘請專業評估師或獨立財務
to the Company according to objective criteria, and if necessary, it      顧問提供諮詢意見。與關連人士有任何利害關
will hire professional appraisers or independent financial advisers to    係的董事，在董事會就該事項表決時，應當迴
provide advice. A Director who has any interest in the connected          避。如有需要，須成立獨立董事委員會，評估
parties shall abstain when the Board votes on the matter. If necessary,   及批准衝突交易，且委員會成員不得有任何於
an independent board committee shall be established to evaluate and       有關交易或安排中佔有重大利益的獨立非執行
approve the conflict transactions, and the members of the committee       董事。

The Group operates a mandatory provident fund (“MPF”) scheme                本集團根據強制性公積金（「強積金」）計劃條
under rules and regulations of MPF Schemes Ordinance for all its            例之規則及規例為其所有香港僱員設立一項

1. GENERAL INFORMATION                                                  1. 概況資料

 Sunny Optical Technology (Group) Company Limited (the                   舜宇光學科技（集團）有限公司（「本公司」）
 “Company”) was incorporated in the Cayman Islands on 21                 於二零零六年九月二十一日在開曼群島根
 September 2006 as an exempted company under the Companies               據開曼群島公司法第 22 章（一九六一年第
 Act Chapter 22 (Law 3 of 1961 as consolidated and revised,              三條法例，經綜合及修訂，以前稱為公司
 formerly known as Companies Law) of the Cayman Islands and              法）註冊成立為獲豁免公司，其股份自二零
 its shares have been listed on the Stock Exchange of Hong Kong          零七年六月十五日起在香港聯合交易所有
 Limited with effect from 15 June 2007. Its ultimate holding and         限公司上市，最終控股及母公司為舜旭有
 parent company is Sun Xu Limited, a private limited company             限公司，一間於英屬處女群島註冊成立的
 incorporated in the British Virgin Islands. Its ultimate controlling    私人有限公司，而最終控股人士為王文鑒
 party is Mr. Wang Wenjian, also a non-executive director of the         先生，亦為本公司非執行董事。本公司註
 Company. The addresses of the registered office and principal           冊辦事處及主要營業地點的地址乃於年報
 place of business of the Company are disclosed in the Corporate         公司資料一節披露。
 Information section of the annual report.


 The Company is an investment holding company. The Company               本公司為一間投資控股公司。本公司及其
 and its subsidiaries (collectively the “Group”) are principally         附屬公司（統稱「本集團」）主要從事設計、
 engaged in the business of designing, researching and developing,       研究與開發、生產及銷售光學及光學相關
 manufacturing and selling of optical and optical related products       產品與科學儀器業務。
 and scientific instruments.


 The consolidated financial statements are presented in Renminbi         綜合財務報表以人民幣（「人民幣」）呈列，
 (“RMB”), which is also the functional currency of the Company.          人民幣亦為本公司的功能貨幣。

    Business combinations                                                      業務合併


     A business is an integrated set of activities and assets which            業務為一組活動及資產，包括一項資
     includes an input and a substantive process that together                 源投入及一項實質過程，而兩者必須
     significantly contribute to the ability to create outputs. The            對創造產出的能力有重大貢獻。倘收

     significantly contribute to the ability to continue producing             貢獻，則被認為屬獨特或稀缺，或在
     outputs and are considered unique or scarce or cannot be                  無重大成本、努力或持續生產產出能
     replaced without significant cost, effort, or delay in the ability        力出現延遲的情況下不可取代。
     to continue producing outputs.

      Business combinations (Continued)                                         業務合併（續）


      Acquisitions of businesses are accounted for using the                    收購業務採用收購法入賬。業務合併
      acquisition method. The consideration transferred in a                    之轉撥代價按公允值計量，而計算方
      business combination is measured at fair value, which is                  法為本集團所轉讓之資產、本集團對
      calculated as the sum of the acquisition-date fair values of              所收購方原擁有人產生之負債及本集
      the assets transferred by the Group, liabilities incurred by the          團於交換所收購方之控制權發行之股
      Group to the former owners of the acquiree and the equity                 權於收購日期之公允值總額。有關收
      interests issued by the Group in exchange for control of the              購之成本一般於產生時於損益中確認。
      acquiree. Acquisition-related costs are generally recognised in
      profit or loss as incurred.

     than profit or loss and other comprehensive income are not           有關變動導致本集團持有的所有權權

     assets or liabilities. Therefore, if a gain or loss previously           全面收益之損益，則會於出售相關資
     recognised in other comprehensive income by that associate               產或負債時重新分類至損益，當有關
     or would be reclassified to profit or loss on the disposal of            聯營公司出售 ╱ 部分出售後，本集團
     the related assets or liabilities, the Group reclassifies the            將收益或虧損由權益重新分類至損益
     gain or loss from equity to profit or loss (as a reclassification        （作為重新分類調整）。
     adjustment) upon disposal/partial disposal of the relevant
     associate.


     When a group entity transacts with an associate of the Group,            倘一間集團實體與本集團聯營公司交
     profits and losses resulting from the transactions with the              易，與該聯營公司交易所產生之損益
     associate are recognised in the Group’s consolidated financial           僅在有關聯營公司之權益與本集團無
     statements only to the extent of interests in the associate that         關的情況下，方會在本集團綜合財務
     are not related to the Group.                                            報表確認。

      Contract costs                                                            合約成本


      Incremental costs of obtaining a contract                                 獲取合約的增量成本


      Incremental costs of obtaining a contract are those costs                 獲取合約的增量成本是本集團為獲取
      that the Group incurs to obtain a contract with a customer                客戶合約而產生的成本，即如果沒有
      that it would not have incurred if the contract had not been              獲得合約，將不會產生有關成本。
      obtained.


      The Group recognises such costs (sales commissions) as                    倘 本 集 團 預 期 收 回 該 等 成 本（ 銷 售 佣
      an asset if it expects to recover these costs. The asset so               金），則將有關成本確認為資產。由此
      recognised is subsequently amortised to profit or loss on a               確認的資產隨後按轉讓與資產相關的
      systematic basis that is consistent with the transfer to the              商品或服務予客戶一致的系統性基準
      customer of the goods or services to which the assets relate.             於損益攤銷。



      The Group applies the practical expedient of expensing all                倘該等成本原本將在一年內悉數於損
      incremental costs to obtain a contract if these costs would               益攤銷，則本集團會應用可行權宜方
      otherwise have been fully amortised to profit or loss within              法支付所有取得合約的增量成本。
      one year.

    Leases                                                               租賃


     Definition of a lease                                               租賃的定義


     A contract is, or contains, a lease if the contract conveys the     倘合約為換取代價而給予在一段時間
     right to control the use of an identified asset for a period of     內控制可識別資產使用的權利，則該
     time in exchange for consideration.                                 合約是租賃或包含租賃。


     For contracts entered into or modified on or after the date         就於首次應用日期或之後訂立或修改
     of initial application or arising from business combinations,       或由業務合併所引致的合約而言，本
     the Group assesses whether a contract is or contains a              集團根據香港財務報告準則第16 號的
     lease based on the definition under HKFRS 16 at inception,          定 義 於 初 始 、 修 改 或 收 購 日 期（ 倘 適
     modification date or acquisition date, as appropriate.              用 ）評 估 該 合 約 是 否 為 租 賃 或 包 含 租
     Such contract will not be reassessed unless the terms and           賃。除非合約的條款及條件其後出現
     conditions of the contract are subsequently changed.                變動，否則有關合約將不予重新評估。


     The Group as a lessee                                               本集團作為承租人


    Short-term leases and leases of low-value assets                     短期租賃及低價值資產租賃


     The Group applies the short-term lease recognition exemption        對於租期自開始日期起計為12 個月或
     to leases of some temporary staff dormitories that have a           以內且並無包含購買選擇權的部分臨
     lease term of 12 months or less from the commencement               時員工宿舍租賃，本集團應用短期租
     date and do not contain a purchase option. It also applies the      賃確認豁免。本集團亦對低價值資產
     recognition exemption for lease of low-value assets. Lease          租賃應用確認豁免。短期租賃及低價
     payments on short-term leases and leases of low-value assets        值資產租賃的租賃付款按直線基準或
     are recognised as expense on a straight-line basis or another       另一系統化基準於租期內確認為開支。
     systematic basis over the lease term.

    Classification and measurement of leases                               租賃的分類及計量


     Leases for which the Group is a lessor are classified as              本集團作為出租人的租賃分類為融資
     finance or operating leases. Whenever the terms of the lease          租賃或經營租賃。當租賃的條款實質
     transfer substantially all the risks and rewards incidental to        上將與相關資產所有權相關的所有風
     ownership of an underlying asset to the lessee, the contract is       險及回報轉讓予承租人時，該項合同
     classified as a finance lease. All other leases are classified as     被分類為融資租賃。所有其他租賃應
     operating leases.                                                     分類為經營租賃。


     Rental income from operating leases is recognised in profit           經營租賃之租金收入於相關租期內以
     or loss on a straight-line basis over the term of the relevant        直線法在損益中確認。於協商及安排
     lease. Initial direct costs incurred in negotiating and arranging     經營租賃時引致之初步直接成本乃加
     an operating lease are added to the carrying amount of the            至租賃資產之賬面值，有關成本於租
     leased asset, and such costs are recognised as an expense             賃期內按直線法確認為開支。
     on a straight-line basis over the lease term.


    Refundable rental deposits                                             可退回租賃按金


     Refundable rental deposits received are accounted for under           已收可退回租賃按金按香港財務報告
     HKFRS 9 and initially measured at fair value. Adjustments to          準則第9 號入賬，並初步按公允值計
     fair value at initial recognition are considered as additional        量。初始確認時對公允值的調整被視
     lease payments from lessees.                                          為承租人的額外租賃付款。

      Lease modification                                                        租賃修改


      Changes in considerations of lease contracts that were not                不屬於原始條款和條件部分的租賃合
      part of the original terms and conditions are accounted for               約的變更將作為租賃修改入賬，包括
      as lease modifications, including lease incentives provided               透過諒解或減租提供租賃優惠。
      through forgiveness or reduction of rentals.


      The Group accounts for a modification to an operating lease               本集團自修改生效之日起將經營租賃
      as a new lease from the effective date of the modification,               的變更作為新租賃入賬，並將與原始
      considering any prepaid or accrued lease payments relating                租賃有關的任何預付款或應計租賃付
      to the original lease as part of the lease payments for the new           款作為新租賃的租賃付款的一部分。
      lease.


      For rent concession under which the Group legally releases                就租金寬免而言，本集團合法解除承
      the lessee from its obligation to make specifically identified            租人義務以明確具體租賃付款，其中
      lease payment, of which some of these lease payments are                  一些為合約到期尚未支付的租賃付款
      contractually due but not paid and some of them are not yet               而另一些為合約尚未到期的租賃付
      contractually due, the Group accounts for the portions which              款，本集團通過應用預期信貸虧損及
      have been recognised as operating lease receivables (i.e. the             香港財務報告準則第9號取消確認要求
      lease payments which are contractually due but not paid)                  將已確認為經營租賃應收款項（即合約
      by applying the ECL and derecognition requirements under                  到期尚未支付的租賃付款）的部分入賬
      HKFRS 9 and applies lease modification requirements for the               並對本集團於實際修訂日期尚未確認
      forgiven lease payments that the Group has not recognised                 的豁免租賃付款（即合約尚未到期的租
      (i.e. the lease payments which are not yet contractually due)             賃付款）應用租賃修訂要求。
      as at the effective date of modification.

    Foreign currencies                                                     外幣


     In preparing the financial statements of each individual              編製各集團實體之財務報表時，以該

     of transactions. At the end of the reporting period, monetary         當日之匯率重新換算。按公允值列賬

     at fair value that are denominated in foreign currencies are          按歷史成本計算之非貨幣項目不予重
     retranslated at the rates prevailing on the date when fair value      新換算。
     was determined. Non-monetary items that are measured
     in terms of historical cost in a foreign currency are not
     retranslated.

     items and on the retranslation of monetary items, are                 所產生的匯兌差額於產生期間的損益
     recognised in profit or loss in the period in which they arise.       確認。

     the Group (i.e. RMB) at exchange rate prevailing at the end           匯率進行折算，除非匯率於該期間波
     of each reporting period. Income and expense items are                動較大，在此情況下，將使用交易日
     translated at the average exchange rates for the period unless        期的匯率。收入和費用按期內的平均

      Borrowing costs                                                           借貸成本


      Borrowing costs directly attributable to the acquisition,                 因收購、興建或生產合資格資產（即需
      construction or production of qualifying assets, which are                相當長時間方可作擬定用途或出售的
      assets that necessarily take a substantial period of time to get          資產）而直接產生的借貸成本歸入該等
      ready for their intended use or sale, are added to the cost of            資產的成本，直至資產基本上可作擬
      those assets until such time as the assets are substantially              定用途或出售為止。
      ready for their intended use or sale.


      All other borrowing costs are recognised in profit or loss in the         所有其他借貸成本均於產生期間於損
      period in which they are incurred.                                        益中確認。


      Government grants                                                         政府補助


      Government grants are not recognised until there is                       政府補助於出現合理保證本集團將會
      reasonable assurance that the Group will comply with the                  遵守附帶的條件及收取補助前，均不
      conditions attaching to them and that the grants will be                  會確認。
      received.


      Government grants are recognised in profit or loss on                     政府補助於本集團確認該等補助擬作
      a systematic basis over the periods in which the Group                    補償之有關成本為開支之期間內按系
      recognises as expenses the related costs for which the                    統基準在損益中確認。尤其是，以本
      grants are intended to compensate. Specifically, government               集團應收購、建造或以其他方式取得
      grants whose primary condition is that the Group should                   非流動資產為主要條件的政府補助，
      purchase, construct or otherwise acquire non-current assets               乃於綜合財務狀況表中確認為遞延收
      are recognised as deferred income in the consolidated                     入，並按系統及合理基準於有關資產
      statement of financial position and transferred to profit or loss         的可用年期內轉至損益。
      on a systematic and rational basis over the useful lives of the
      related assets.


      Government grants related to income that are receivable as                作為已產生開支或虧損之補償或向本
      compensation for expenses or losses already incurred or for               集團提供即時財務支持而無日後相關
      the purpose of giving immediate financial support to the Group            成本而應收與收入有關之政府補助，
      with no future related costs are recognised in profit or loss in          於其應收取期間在損益中確認。此補
      the period in which they become receivable. Such grants are               助呈列於「其他收益」項下。
      presented under “other income”.

    Taxation                                                                 稅項


     Income tax expense represents the sum of the tax currently              所得稅開支指即期應付稅項與遞延稅
     payable and deferred tax.                                               項的總和。


     The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit for the            即期應付稅項根據本年度應課稅溢利
     year. Taxable profit differs from profit before tax as reported         計算。應課稅溢利與綜合損益及其他
     in the consolidated statement of profit or loss and other               全面收益表所報告的除稅前溢利不
     comprehensive income because of income or expense that                  同，此乃因其他年度的應課稅或可扣
     are taxable or deductible in other years and items that are             稅收支項目，以及毋須課稅或不可扣
     never taxable or deductible. The Group’s liability for current          稅項目所致。本集團的即期稅項負債
     tax is calculated using tax rates that have been enacted or             根據截至報告期末已頒佈或實質頒佈
     substantively enacted by the end of the reporting period.               的稅率計算。


     Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences                     遞延稅項按綜合財務報表中資產及負
     between the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities in the           債賬面值與計算應課稅溢利所採用相
     consolidated financial statements and the corresponding tax             應稅基之暫時差額確認。遞延稅項負
     bases used in the computation of taxable profit. Deferred tax           債一般就所有應課稅暫時差額確認入
     liabilities are generally recognised for all taxable temporary          賬。遞延稅項資產一般就所有暫時差
     differences. Deferred tax assets are generally recognised for           額確認入賬，惟可能有應課稅溢利用
     all temporary differences to the extent that it is probable that        於對銷可扣稅暫時差額。若於一項交
     taxable profits will be available against which those deductible        易 中 ， 因 初 次 確 認 資 產 及 負 債（ 業 務
     temporary differences can be utilised. Such deferred tax                合併除外）而引致之暫時差額既不影響
     assets and liabilities are not recognised if the temporary              應課稅溢利亦不影響會計溢利，則不
     difference arises from the initial recognition (other than in a         會確認該等遞延稅項資產及負債。此
     business combination) of assets and liabilities in a transaction        外，倘初步確認商譽產生暫時差額，
     that affects neither the taxable profit nor the accounting              遞延稅項負債不會確認。
     profit. In addition, deferred tax liabilities are not recognised if
     the temporary difference arises from the initial recognition of
     goodwill.

      future. Deferred tax assets arising from deductible temporary             投資的可扣減暫時差額而產生的遞延

     comprehensive income or directly in equity, in which case,           益中確認之項目相關，則即期及遞延
     the current and deferred tax are also recognised in other            稅項亦分別於其他全面收益或直接於
     comprehensive income or directly in equity respectively. Where       權益中確認。倘因業務合併之初步會
     current tax or deferred tax arises from the initial accounting       計方法而產生即期或遞延稅項，有關
     for a business combination, the tax effect is included in the        稅務影響會計入業務合併之會計方法
     accounting for the business combination.                             內。

      necessary for it to be capable of operating in the manner                 及條件而直接產生的任何成本（包括測
      intended by management, including costs of testing whether                試 相 關 資 產 是 否 正 常 運 行 之 成 本 ）及
      the related assets are functioning properly and, for qualifying           （對合資格資產而言）根據本集團會計

      the assets are ready for their intended use.

  3.2 Significant accounting policies (Continued)                       3.2 重要會計政策（續）

    Cash and cash equivalents                                            現金及現金等值項目


     Cash and cash equivalents presented on the consolidated             現金及現金等價物於綜合財務狀況表
     statement of financial position include:                            呈列，包括：


     (a) cash, which comprises of cash on hand and demand                (a) 現 金 ， 其 包 括 手 頭 現 金 及 活 期 存
         deposits, excluding bank balances that are subject to              款，不包括受監管限制而導致有關
         regulatory restrictions that result in such balances no            結餘不再符合現金定義的銀行結
         longer meeting the definition of cash; and                         餘；及


     (b) cash equivalents, which comprises of short-term (generally      (b) 現金等值項目，其包括短期（通常
         with original maturity of three months or less), highly            原到期日為三個月或更短）、可隨
         liquid investments that are readily convertible to a known         時轉換為已知數額現金且價值變動
         amount of cash and which are subject to an insignificant           風險不大的高流動性投資。現金等
         risk of changes in value. Cash equivalents are held for the        值項目持作滿足短期現金承擔，而
         purpose of meeting short-term cash commitments rather              非用於投資或其他目的。
         than for investment or other purposes.


    Inventories                                                          存貨


     Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net realisable      存貨按成本及可變現淨值的較低者列
     value. Costs of inventories are determined on a weighted            賬。存貨成本按加權平均法釐定。可
     average method. Net realisable value represents the estimated       變現淨值指存貨估計售價減直至完工
     selling price for inventories less all estimated costs of           的全部估計成本及銷售活動所需成
     completion and costs necessary to make the sale. Costs              本。銷售活動所需成本包括銷售活動
     necessary to make the sale include incremental costs directly       直接產生的增量成本及集團銷售活動
     attributable to the sale and non-incremental costs which the        必須產生的非增量成本。
     Group must incur to make the sale.

      Financial instruments                                                     金融工具


      Financial assets and financial liabilities are recognised when            當集團實體成為工具合約條文的訂約
      a group entity becomes a party to the contractual provisions              方時，金融資產及金融負債方獲確
      of the instrument. All regular way purchases or sales of                  認。所有以正規途徑買賣之金融資產
      financial assets are recognised and derecognised on a trade               乃按交易日期基準確認及取消確認。
      date basis. Regular way purchases or sales are purchases or               正規途徑買賣乃要求於市場法規或慣
      sales of financial assets that require delivery of assets within          例所確定之時間框架內交付資產之金
      the time frame established by regulation or convention in the             融資產買賣。
      market place.


      Financial assets and financial liabilities are initially measured at      金融資產及金融負債初步按公允值計
      fair value except for trade receivables arising from contracts            量，惟根據香港財務報告準則第15 號
      with customers which are initially measured in accordance                 初步計量的與客戶合約產生的貿易應
      with HKFRS 15. Transaction costs that are directly attributable           收款項除外。交易成本直接歸屬於收
      to the acquisition or issue of financial assets and financial             購或發行金融資產及金融負債（按公允
      liabilities (other than financial assets or financial liabilities at      值計入損益的金融資產或金融負債除
      FVTPL) are added to or deducted from the fair value of the                外），在初始確認時適當地計入或自金
      financial assets or financial liabilities, as appropriate, on initial     融資產或金融負債的公允值中扣除。
      recognition. Transaction costs directly attributable to the               直接歸屬於收購按公允值計入損益的
      acquisition of financial assets or financial liabilities at FVTPL         金融資產或金融負債的交易成本，即
      are recognised immediately in profit or loss.                             時於損益中確認。


      The effective interest method is a method of calculating                  實際利率法為計算金融資產或金融負
      the amortised cost of a financial asset or financial liability            債的攤銷成本以及於相關期間內分配
      and of allocating interest income and interest expense over               利息收入及利息支出的方法。實際利
      the relevant period. The effective interest rate is the rate              率指在金融資產或金融負債的預計年
      that exactly discounts estimated future cash receipts and                 期內或（如適用）較短期間內準確折算
      payments (including all fees and points paid or received that             估 計 未 來 現 金 收 入 及 支 出（ 包 括 構 成
      form an integral part of the effective interest rate, transaction         實際利率整體部分的所有已付或已收
      costs and other premiums or discounts) through the expected               的費用及點數、交易成本及其他溢價
      life of the financial asset or financial liability, or, where             或折扣）至初步確認時的賬面淨值的利
      appropriate, a shorter period to the net carrying amount on               率。
      initial recognition.

     9. The amount of ECL is updated at each reporting date to                            進行減值評估，其須根據香港財務報

                                                                                          金額於各報告日期更新，以反映自初

     Lifetime ECL represents the ECL that will result from all                            全期預期信貸虧損指於相關工具預期
     possible default events over the expected life of the relevant                       壽命內發生所有可能的違約事件而導
     i n s t r u m e n t . I n c o n t r a s t , 12- m o n t h E C L ( “12m E C L ” )     致的預期信貸虧損。相反，12 個月預
     represents the portion of lifetime ECL that is expected to                           期信貸虧損（「12 個月預期信貸虧損」）
     result from default events that are possible within 12 months                        則指預期於報告日期後12 個月內可能
     after the reporting date. Assessments are done based on the                          發生的違約事件而導致的部分全期預
     Group’s historical credit loss experience, adjusted for factors                      期信貸虧損。預期信貸虧損根據本集
     that are specific to the debtors, general economic conditions                        團過往信貸虧損經驗進行評估，並根
     and an assessment of both the current conditions at the                              據債務人的特定因素、一般經濟狀況
     reporting date as well as the forecast of future conditions.                         及於報告日期對當前狀況及未來狀況
                                                                                          預測的評估而作出調整。

           events that have a detrimental impact on the estimated                 未來現金流量產生不利影響的事件

           Evidence that a financial asset is credit-impaired includes            值。金融資產出現信貸減值的證據

                 event;                                                              行為；

                 financial difficulty, having granted to the borrower a              給予借款人在一般情況下不會

                 consider;

    (iv) Write-off policy                                               (iv) 撇銷政策


         The Group writes off a financial asset when there is              倘有資料顯示交易對手處於嚴重財
         information indicating that the counterparty is in severe         務困難及無實際期望可收回欠款，
         financial difficulty and there is no realistic prospect of        本集團則撇銷該金融資產（例如於
         recovery, for example, when the counterparty has been             交易對手被清盤或進入破產程序
         placed under liquidation or has entered into bankruptcy           時）。金融資產撇銷仍受限於本集
         proceedings. Financial assets written off may still be            團收回欠款程序下的執行活動，且
         subject to enforcement activities under the Group’s               在適當情況下將參考法律意見。撇
         recovery procedures, taking into account legal advice             銷構成一項終止確認事項，任何其
         where appropriate. A write-off constitutes a derecognition        後收回的欠款於損益內確認。
         event. Any subsequent recoveries are recognised in profit
         or loss.

      (v) Measurement and recognition of ECL                                    (v) 預期信貸虧損的計量及確認


           The measurement of ECL is a function of the probability                預期信貸虧損計量是違約可能性、
           of default, loss given default (i.e. the magnitude of the              違約損失率（即發生違約時的損失
           loss if there is a default) and the exposure at default.               程度）及違約風險的函數。違約可
           The assessment of the probability of default and loss                  能性及違約損失率的評定乃基於經
           given default is based on historical data adjusted and                 調整歷史數據及前瞻性資料。預期
           forward-looking information. Estimation of ECL reflects                信貸虧損的估算乃無偏頗及概率加
           an unbiased and probability-weighted amount that is                    權的數額，其按相應違約風險的權
           determined with the respective risks of default occurring              重釐定。本集團經考慮過往信貸虧
           as the weights. The Group uses a practical expedient in                損經驗後使用撥備矩陣並採用實際
           estimating ECL on trade receivables using a provision                  權益法估計貿易應收款項的預期信
           matrix taking into consideration historical credit loss                貸虧損，並按無需花費不必要成本
           experience, adjusted for forward looking information that              或精力可取得的前瞻性資料調整。
           is available without undue cost or effort.


           Generally, the ECL is the difference between all                       一般而言，預期信貸虧損為根據合
           contractual cash flows that are due to the Group in                    約應付本集團的所有合約現金流量
           accordance with the contract and the cash flows that                   與本集團預期將收取的現金流量之
           the Group expects to receive, discounted at the effective              間的差額，並按初步確認時釐定的
           interest rate determined at initial recognition.                       實際利率貼現。

     The Group derecognises a financial asset only when the               本集團僅於資產產生的現金流量的合
     contractual rights to the cash flows from the asset expire, or       約權利到期時，或於其轉移金融資產
     when it transfers the financial asset and substantially all the      並同時轉移持有該等資產絕大部分的
     risks and rewards of ownership of the asset to another entity.       風險及回報於另一實體時終止確認金
     If the Group neither transfers nor retains substantially all the     融資產。倘本集團並未轉讓或保留擁

     transferred asset, the Group recognises its retained interest        控制已轉讓資產，本集團確認其於資
     in the asset and an associated liability for amounts it may          產的保留權益以及其可能需要支付的
     have to pay. If the Group retains substantially all the risks        相關負債金額。倘本集團保留已轉讓
     and rewards of ownership of a transferred financial asset,           金融資產擁有權的絕大部分風險與回
     the Group continues to recognise the financial asset and             報，則本集團繼續確認金融資產，亦
     also recognises a collateralised borrowing for the proceeds          就已收所得款項確認已抵押借貸。
     received.


     On derecognition of a financial asset measured at amortised          於終止確認按攤銷成本計量的金融資
     cost, the difference between the asset’s carrying amount             產時，資產賬面值與已收及應收代價
     and the sum of the consideration received and receivable is          總額間的差額於損益中確認。
     recognised in profit or loss.


     On derecognition of the receivables that are measured at             於終止確認按公允值計入其他全面收
     FVTOCI, the cumulative gain or loss previously accumulated in        益計量的應收款項時，先前於按公允
     the FVTOCI reserve is reclassified to profit or loss.                值計入其他全面收益列賬的儲備累計
                                                                          的累計盈虧重新分類至損益。


     On derecognition of an investment in equity instrument which         於終止確認本集團選擇於初步確認時
     the Group has elected on initial recognition to measure at           按公允值計入其他全面收益計量的權

     is recognised in profit or loss on the purchase, sale, issue or           行或註銷本公司本身的權益工具概不
     cancellation of the Company’s own equity instruments.                     會於損益中確認收益或虧損。

   UNCERTAINTY

  In the application of the Group’s accounting policies, which are         於應用附註3所述本集團之會計政策時，本
  described in Note 3, the directors of the Company are required to        公司董事須就未能於其他來源取得之資產
  make judgements, estimates and assumptions about the carrying            及負債賬面值作出判斷、估計及假設。該
  amounts of assets and liabilities that are not readily apparent          等估計及有關假設乃根據過往經驗及被視
  from other sources. The estimates and underlying assumptions             為相關之其他因素而作出。實際結果或有
  are based on historical experience and other factors that are            別於該等估計。
  considered to be relevant. Actual results may differ from these
  estimates.


  The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed on an on-          估計及相關假設將受到持續檢討。倘會計
  going basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are recognised in         估計修訂只影響該期間，則有關會計估計
  the period in which the estimate is revised if the revision affects      的修訂會在修訂估計期間確認，或倘有關
  only that period, or in the period of the revision and future periods    修訂同時影響當前及未來期間，則有關修
  if the revision affects both current and future periods.                 訂會在修訂期間及未來期間確認。


  The following are the key assumptions concerning the future,             以下為於報告期末很可能導致下一個財政
  and other key sources of estimation uncertainty at the end of            年度資產及負債賬面值出現重大調整的有
  the reporting period that may have a significant risk of causing         關將來之主要假設及其他估計不確定因素

  liabilities within the next financial year.

 Provision of ECL for trade receivables                                   貿易應收款項的預期信貸虧損撥備

 The Group uses provision matrix to calculate ECL for the trade           本集團使用撥備矩陣以計算並無出現信貸
 receivables which are not credit-impaired. The provision matrix          減值的貿易應收款項的預期信貸虧損。撥
 is based on debtor’s aging as groupings of various debtors that          備矩陣乃基於債務人按各債務人類似虧損
 have similar loss patterns. The provision rate is based on the           模式將其分組的賬齡。撥備率乃基於本集
 Group’s historical observed default rates taking into consideration      團過往觀察的逾期率，並考慮無需付出過
 the forward-looking information that is reasonable and supportable       多成本或工作即可取的合理及可支持的前
 available without undue costs or effort. At every reporting date,        瞻性資料。於每個報告日期，過往觀察的
 the historical observed default rates are reassessed and changes         違約率會重新評估，並會考慮前瞻性資料
 in the forward-looking information are considered. In addition,          的變動。此外，出現信貸減值的貿易應收
 trade receivables which are credit impaired are assessed for ECL         款項的預期信貸虧損將獨立地評估。
 individually.


 The provision of ECL is sensitive to changes in estimates. The           預期信貸虧損撥備對估算變動敏感。有關
 information about the ECL and the Group’s trade receivables are          預期信貸虧損及本集團貿易應收款項的資
 disclosed in Note 37(b) and Note 25 respectively.                        料分別於附註37(b)及附註25披露。

36. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                                36. 資本風險管理
  The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the Group           本集團管理其資本，以確保本集團轄下實
  will be able to continue as a going concern while maximising the             體能夠持續經營，同時通過優化債務及權
  return to shareholders through the optimisation of the debt and              益結構，實現股東回報最大化。本集團的
  equity balances. The Group’s overall strategy remains unchanged              整體策略與去年相同。
  from prior year.


  The capital structure of the Group consists of net debt, which               本集團的資本架構由負債淨額（包括於附
  includes the bank borrowings disclosed in Note 31, lease liabilities         註31中披露的銀行借貸、於附註32中披露
  disclosed in Note 32 and bonds payable disclosed in Note 34 net              的租賃負債及於附註34 中披露的應付債券
  of cash and cash equivalents and equity attributable to owners              （扣除現金及現金等值項目））及本公司股東
  of the Company, comprising issued share capital, retained profits            應佔權益（包括已發行股本、保留盈利及其
  and other reserves.                                                          他儲備）組成。


  The directors of the Company review the capital structure on a               本公司董事每半年檢討一次資本架構。作
  semi-annual basis. As part of this review, the directors of the              為檢討的一部分，本公司董事考慮資本成
  Company consider the cost of capital and the risks associated                本及各類資本相關風險。根據本公司董事
  with each class of capital. Based on recommendations of the                  的建議，本集團將透過支付股息、發行新
  directors of the Company, the Group will balance its overall capital         股及股份回購以及發行新債或償還現有債
  structure through the payment of dividends, new share issues                 務來平衡其整體資本架構。
  and share buy-backs as well as the issue of new debts or the
  repayment of existing debts.

    Interest rate benchmark reform                                        利率基準改革


    A fundamental reform of major interest rate benchmarks                主要利率基準已於全球範圍內進行根
    has been undertaken globally to replace some interbank                本性改革，以近乎無風險利率替代若
    offered rates (“IBORs”) with alternative nearly risk-free rates.      干銀行同業拆息（「銀行同業拆息」）。
    The Group is closely monitoring the market and managing               本集團正在密切監察市場及設法向新
    the transition to new benchmark interest rates, including             基準利率（包括相關銀行同業拆息監管
    announcements made by the relevant IBOR regulators.                   者公佈的利率）過渡。


    HIBOR                                                                 香港銀行同業拆息利率


    While the Hong Kong Dollar Overnight Index Average                    儘管港元隔夜平均指數（「港元隔夜平
    (“HONIA”) has been identified as an alternative to HIBOR,             均 指 數 」）已 獲 識 別 為 香 港 銀 行 同 業
    there is no plan to discontinue HIBOR. The multi-rate                 拆息利率的替代方案，惟並無終止香
    approach has been adopted in Hong Kong, whereby HIBOR                 港銀行同業拆息利率的計劃。香港一
    and HONIA will co-exist. The Group’s bank borrowings linked           直採納多利率方針，據此，香港銀行
    to HIBOR will continue till maturity and hence, not subject to        同業拆息利率及港元隔夜平均指數將
    transition.                                                           會共存。本集團與香港銀行同業拆息

                                                                          止，因此不會受到過渡的影響。

43. SHARE AWARD SCHEME                                                        43. 股份獎勵計劃

   The Company adopted The Sunny Optical Technology (Group)                    本公司已於二零一零年三月二十二日（「採
   Company Limited Restricted Share Award Scheme (the                          納日期」）採納了舜宇光學科技（集團）有限
   “Restricted Share Award Scheme”) on 22 March 2010                           公司限制性股份獎勵計劃（「限制性股份獎
   (“Adoption Date”) with a duration of 10 years commencing                    勵計劃」），該計劃自採納日期起持續十年
   from the Adoption Date. The objective of the Restricted Share               有效。限制性股份獎勵計劃旨在為特選參
   Award Scheme is to provide the selected participants including              與者提供（包括本公司董事、僱員、本公司
   directors of the Company, employees, agents or consultants of               及其附屬公司的代理或顧問）
                                                                                           （「特選參與
   the Company and its subsidiaries (the “Selected Participants”)              者」）一個購買本公司所有人權益的機會；
   with an opportunity to acquire a proprietary interest in the                鼓勵及挽留於本集團工作的個別人士；及
   Company; to encourage and retain such individuals to work with              額外推動彼等爭取達到業績目標，以實現
   the Group; and to provide additional incentive for them to achieve          提升本公司價值，並且透過擁有股份，使
   performance goals, with a view to achieving the objectives of               特選參與者的權益與本公司股東的權益直
   increasing the value of the Company and aligning the interests              接掛鈎的目標。本集團已成立舜宇光學科
   of the Selected Participants directly to be the owners of the               技（集團）有限公司限制性股份獎勵計劃信
   Company through ownership of shares. The Group has set up The               託（「信託」）以管理及持有本公司股份直至
   Sunny Optical Technology (Group) Company Limited Restricted                 股份歸屬及轉讓予特選參與者。在向特選
   Share Award Scheme Trust (the “Trust”) to administrate and hold             參與者授出股份時，信託在公開市場上購
   the Company’s shares before they are vested and transferred                 買將予授出之本公司股份，並由本公司以
